> DEVELOPER

Let's think about the Series feature. I want Series to support collections; a Series should be a collection of posts that might come from other Series or the posts folder. Considering SEO and comments, the post URL cannot change, but the sidebar needs to be the collection's Series menu. Even the post title might need to change to match the new collection. Is this a good idea? What is your opinion?

> AGENT

This is an interesting design challenge. Let me share my thoughts honestly.

## The Core Tension

You're trying to solve a real problem: curated reading paths that cross-cut existing organizational boundaries (e.g., a "Best of 2025" or "Learning Rust" collection that pulls from scattered series and posts). That's a legitimate use case.

But the design as described has a fundamental ambiguity problem:

**If the URL doesn't change, how does the system know which sidebar to render?**

A post at `/posts/my-post` could belong to Series A and Collection B. When a reader arrives, there's no signal to choose between them. You'd need either:
1. A default (probably the post's own series) — defeating the collection's sidebar purpose
2. Referrer detection — fragile and doesn't survive sharing/bookmarking
3. A query param like `?collection=slug` — the cleanest option

## Title Overrides Are a Bad Idea

A post with two titles in different contexts is confusing for users who share or bookmark. The `<title>` tag can only carry one value — collection-context title overrides would only work as UI chrome, not actual metadata. I'd drop this.

## A Better Model: Collections via Query Param

Rather than fighting URL immutability, lean into it:

- A Collection's index page lists posts from any source (by slug reference)
- Navigation within a collection propagates `?collection=slug` in prev/next links
- At the post level, if `?collection=slug` is present, render the collection's sidebar instead of the post's own series sidebar
- Canonical `<link>` tag strips the query param (SEO safe)
- Collection frontmatter defines an ordered list of post slugs:

```yaml
type: collection
posts:
  - series: "rust-basics"
  - slug: "my-standalone-post"
  - series: "async-rust"
    exclude: ["async-intro"]
```

## Simpler Alternative

If you don't want query param complexity: collections are **index-only** — the collection page lists and links to posts. Posts render normally with their own series context. This is how most blogs handle it (e.g., "tag" or "reading list" pages). Lower complexity, zero ambiguity.

---

**My recommendation**: Start with the simpler index-only model. If users clearly need guided sequential reading through collections (with prev/next), add the query param approach. The title override idea adds complexity without clear SEO or UX benefit — skip it.

What's the primary use case you're trying to solve? That would help narrow down which approach fits best.

> AGENT

Now I have a full picture. Here's my analysis and recommendation.

## Location: Yes, `content/series/`

It's the right call. Collections are semantically a type of curated reading sequence. Keeping them in `content/series/` means:
- `getSeriesData` / `getSeriesPosts` extend naturally
- The `/series/[slug]` index page works for collections too
- `site.config.ts`'s `series.navbar` and `series.customPaths` apply without special-casing

A `type: collection` field in frontmatter distinguishes them from regular series.

## Query Param Slug: Yes, the folder name

`content/series/rust-learning/index.mdx` → `?collection=rust-learning`. Consistent with how series slugs work everywhere else.

## Posts List Format

The existing `posts:` field is a flat array of slug strings. For a collection you need to reference both individual posts and entire series. I'd use a separate `items:` key (not reuse `posts:`) to keep the formats unambiguous and avoid detection hacks:

```yaml
---
type: collection
title: "Learning Rust"
excerpt: "A curated path through Rust from basics to async."
date: "2026-01-01"
featured: true
items:
  - series: rust-basics
  - slug: my-standalone-rust-post
  - series: async-rust
    exclude:
      - async-intro
---
```

Each item is either:
- `{ series: "slug" }` — include all posts from that series in their series-defined order
- `{ series: "slug", exclude: ["post-slug"] }` — include all except listed
- `{ slug: "post-slug" }` — include a single standalone post

This maps cleanly to a resolver function:

```ts
function getCollectionPosts(collectionSlug: string): PostData[] {
  const data = getSeriesData(collectionSlug);
  // if type !== 'collection', fall back to existing getSeriesPosts logic
  return (data.items ?? []).flatMap(item => {
    if ('series' in item) {
      const posts = getSeriesPosts(item.series);
      return posts.filter(p => !item.exclude?.includes(p.slug));
    }
    return getPostBySlug(item.slug) ? [getPostBySlug(item.slug)!] : [];
  });
}
```

## On Title Overrides (revisited)

Since you raised it again: a lightweight middle ground is a `label:` field per item, used **only in the sidebar nav**, not in `<title>` or headings:

```yaml
items:
  - series: rust-basics
  - slug: my-standalone-rust-post
    label: "Putting It Together"   # overrides sidebar display name only
```

This avoids SEO issues entirely while letting you customize how the post appears within the collection's context.

---

**Summary of the schema:**

| Field | Type | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `type: collection` | string | Marks as collection, not regular series |
| `items` | array | Ordered list of series/post references |
| `items[].series` | string | Include an entire series |
| `items[].slug` | string | Include a single post by slug |
| `items[].exclude` | string[] | Posts to skip (with `series:`) |
| `items[].label` | string | Sidebar display name override (optional) |

Does this shape match what you had in mind?

> DEVELOPER

Yes, much better. Using 'slug' in the items list seems odd; would 'post' be better, or can you think of a better word? Also, what would the collection series and post URLs look like?

> AGENT

## `post:` vs `slug:`

`post:` is better. It's a domain term, not an implementation detail, and it creates a clean symmetric pair:

```yaml
items:
  - series: rust-basics
  - post: my-standalone-rust-post
  - series: async-rust
    exclude:
      - async-intro
  - post: my-other-post
    label: "Putting It Together"
```

`series: ...` / `post: ...` reads naturally and is unambiguous. Both are what they say they are.

## URLs

**Collection index page** — same pattern as regular series, no special treatment needed:
```
/series/rust-learning
```

**Post viewed in collection context** — canonical URL unchanged, collection context carried by query param:
```
/posts/my-standalone-rust-post?collection=rust-learning
/series/rust-basics/intro-to-ownership?collection=rust-learning
```

The post's native URL is always the canonical URL. The `?collection=` param is only present when navigating via the collection's prev/next links. If someone shares or bookmarks a URL without the param, they see the post's normal sidebar — which is fine.

**Practical navigation flow:**
1. User lands on `/series/rust-learning` (collection index)
2. Clicks "Start Reading" → `/posts/my-standalone-rust-post?collection=rust-learning`
3. Sidebar shows the collection's menu, not the post's own series
4. Prev/Next buttons stay within the collection, propagating `?collection=rust-learning`
5. If user copies the URL and opens it directly without the param, they see the post normally — no broken state

**SEO:** The canonical `<link>` tag strips the query param, so `/posts/my-post` is always the indexed URL. No duplicate content issues.

One thing to decide: should a post's page know statically which collections it belongs to (for generating `<link rel="alternate">` or breadcrumbs)? That would require an index built at static generation time — `Map<postSlug, collectionSlug[]>`. Worth considering but not required for the first iteration.

> DEVELOPER

I agree with you; the post page's knowledge of the collection can be implemented later. besides, Currently, posts in a series and posts outside a series use the same URL style. We will refactor this later, but not right now; we can discuss it later. what do you think?

> AGENT

Agreed completely — deferring is the right call, and here's why it's a clean decision:

The collection feature as designed works **regardless** of the future URL structure. Whether series posts eventually move to `/series/rust-basics/my-post` or stay at `/posts/my-post`, the `?collection=slug` mechanism is orthogonal to that. The query param just says "render this post in collection context" — it doesn't depend on the post's path.

When you do tackle the URL refactor later, the main concerns will be:
- **Redirects**: `/posts/old-slug` → `/series/rust-basics/old-slug` (301s, static export makes this non-trivial)
- **`getPostUrl` in `urls.ts`**: already centralized, so the change surface is contained
- **Canonical tags**: already need to be correct for the collection feature, so that groundwork will be laid

Shipping collections first actually makes the URL refactor slightly easier later — by then you'll have a clear model of "a post knows which series it belongs to" and potentially "which collections reference it", which is useful context for deciding the new URL scheme.

So the plan is solid: **collections now, URL restructuring later as a separate, deliberate change.**

- `items:` list using `series:` and `post:` keys
- `?collection=slug` query param for sidebar context
- Canonical URL unaffected

    20→
    21→const PostSchema = z.object({
    22→  title: z.string(),
    23→  date: z.union([z.string(), z.date()]).transform(val => new Date(val).toISOString().split('T')[0]).optional(),
    24→  subtitle: z.string().optional(),
    25→  excerpt: z.string().optional(),

    27→  tags: z.array(z.string()).optional().default([]),
    28→  authors: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
    29→  author: z.string().optional(),
    30→  layout: z.string().optional().default('post'),
    31→  series: z.string().optional(),
    32→  coverImage: z.string().optional(),
    33→  sort: z.enum(['date-desc', 'date-asc', 'manual']).optional().default('date-desc'),
    34→  posts: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
    35→  featured: z.boolean().optional().default(false),
    36→  pinned: z.boolean().optional().default(false),
    37→  draft: z.boolean().optional().default(false),
    38→  latex: z.boolean().optional().default(false),
    39→  toc: z.boolean().optional().default(true),
    40→  commentable: z.boolean().optional(),
    41→  externalLinks: z.array(ExternalLinkSchema).optional().default([]),
    42→});
    43→
    44→export interface Heading {
    45→  id: string;
    46→  text: string;
    47→  level: number;
    48→}
    49→
    50→export interface ExternalLink {
    51→  name: string;
    52→  url: string;
    53→}
    54→
    55→export interface PostData {
    56→  slug: string;
    57→  title: string;
    58→  subtitle?: string;
    59→  date: string;
    60→  excerpt: string;
    61→  category: string;
    62→  tags: string[];
    63→  authors: string[];
    64→  layout?: string;
    65→  series?: string;
    66→  seriesTitle?: string;
    67→  coverImage?: string;
    68→  sort?: 'date-desc' | 'date-asc' | 'manual';
    69→  posts?: string[];
    70→  featured?: boolean;
    71→  pinned?: boolean;
    72→  draft?: boolean;
    73→  latex?: boolean;
    74→  toc?: boolean;
    75→  commentable?: boolean;
    76→  externalLinks?: ExternalLink[];
    77→  readingTime: string;
    78→  content: string;
    79→  headings: Heading[];
    80→  contentLocales?: Record<string, { content: string; title?: string; excerpt?: string; headings?: Heading[] }>;
    81→}
    82→
    83→export function calculateReadingTime(content: string): string {
    84→  const wordsPerMinute = 200;
    85→  const hanCharsPerMinute = 300;
    86→
    87→  // Strip tags and common markdown syntax before counting.
    88→  const text = content
    89→    .replace(/<\/?[^>]+(>|$)/g, "")
    90→    .replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, "")
    91→    .replace(/`[^`]*`/g, "")
    92→    .replace(/!\[[^\]]*\]\([^)]+\)/g, "")
    93→    .replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\([^)]+\)/g, "$1")
    94→    .replace(/[#*_~>\-[\]()]/g, " ");
    95→
    96→  const hanCharCount = (text.match(/[\u3400-\u4DBF\u4E00-\u9FFF\uF900-\uFAFF]/g) || []).length;
    97→  const latinWordCount = (text.match(/[A-Za-z0-9]+(?:['’-][A-Za-z0-9]+)*/g) || []).length;
    98→
    99→  const estimatedMinutes = (latinWordCount / wordsPerMinute) + (hanCharCount / hanCharsPerMinute);
   100→  const minutes = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(estimatedMinutes));

     1→import { getSeriesData, getSeriesPosts, getAllSeries, resolveSeriesAuthors, getAuthorSlug } from '@/lib/markdown';
     2→import { notFound } from 'next/navigation';
     3→import SeriesCatalog from '@/components/SeriesCatalog';
     4→import Pagination from '@/components/Pagination';
     5→import { Metadata } from 'next';
     6→import { siteConfig } from '../../../../site.config';
     7→import CoverImage from '@/components/CoverImage';
     8→import Link from 'next/link';
     9→import { t, resolveLocale } from '@/lib/i18n';
    10→import { getPostUrl } from '@/lib/urls';
    11→
    12→const PAGE_SIZE = siteConfig.pagination.series;
    13→
    14→export async function generateStaticParams() {
    15→  const allSeries = getAllSeries();
    16→  const slugs = Object.keys(allSeries);
    17→  if (slugs.length === 0) return [{ slug: '_' }];
    18→  return slugs.map((slug) => ({ slug }));
    19→}
    20→
    21→export const dynamicParams = false;
    22→
    23→export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }): Promise<Metadata> {
    24→  const { slug: rawSlug } = await params;
    25→  const slug = decodeURIComponent(rawSlug);
    26→  const seriesData = getSeriesData(slug);
    27→  
    28→  if (!seriesData) {
    29→    // If no explicit series metadata, try to infer from posts or return default
    30→    const posts = getSeriesPosts(slug);
    31→    if (posts.length > 0) {
    32→        return {
    33→            title: `${slug} - ${t('series')} | ${resolveLocale(siteConfig.title)}`,
    34→            description: `${posts.length} ${t('posts').toLowerCase()} - ${slug}.`,
    35→        }
    36→    }
    37→    return { title: 'Series Not Found' };
    38→  }
    39→
    40→  const ogImage = seriesData.coverImage && !seriesData.coverImage.startsWith('text:') && !seriesData.coverImage.startsWith('./')
    41→    ? seriesData.coverImage
    42→    : siteConfig.ogImage;
    43→
    44→  return {
    45→    title: `${seriesData.title} - ${t('series')} | ${resolveLocale(siteConfig.title)}`,
    46→    description: seriesData.excerpt,
    47→    openGraph: {
    48→      title: seriesData.title,
    49→      description: seriesData.excerpt,
    50→      type: 'website',
    51→      url: `${siteConfig.baseUrl}/series/${slug}`,
    52→      siteName: resolveLocale(siteConfig.title),
    53→      images: [{ url: ogImage, width: 1200, height: 630, alt: seriesData.title }],
    54→    },
    55→    twitter: {
    56→      card: ogImage !== siteConfig.ogImage ? 'summary_large_image' : 'summary',
    57→      title: seriesData.title,
    58→      description: seriesData.excerpt,
    59→      images: [ogImage],
    60→    },
    61→  };
    62→}
    63→
    64→export default async function SeriesPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
    65→  const { slug: rawSlug } = await params;
    66→  const slug = decodeURIComponent(rawSlug);
    67→  const seriesData = getSeriesData(slug);
    68→  const allPosts = getSeriesPosts(slug);
    69→
    70→  if ((!seriesData && allPosts.length === 0) || (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' && seriesData?.draft)) {
    71→    notFound();
    72→  }
    73→
    74→  const page = 1;
    75→  const totalPages = Math.ceil(allPosts.length / PAGE_SIZE);
    76→  const posts = allPosts.slice(0, PAGE_SIZE);
    77→
    78→  // Fallback title if seriesData not found (e.g. no index.md but posts exist via frontmatter)
    79→  const title = seriesData?.title || slug.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + slug.slice(1);
    80→  const description = seriesData?.excerpt;
    81→  const coverImage = seriesData?.coverImage;
    82→  const authors = resolveSeriesAuthors(slug, allPosts);
    83→
    84→  return (
    85→    <div className="layout-main">
    86→      <header className="mb-16">
    87→        {/* Cover image hero */}
    88→        {coverImage && (
    89→          <div className="relative w-full h-56 md:h-72 mb-10 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-xl shadow-accent/5">
    90→            <CoverImage
    91→              src={coverImage}
    92→              title={title}
    93→              slug={slug}
    94→              className="w-full h-full object-cover"
    95→            />
    96→            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-background/60 to-transparent" />
    97→          </div>
    98→        )}
    99→
   100→        <div className="text-center max-w-2xl mx-auto">
   101→          <span className="badge-accent mb-4">
   102→            {t('series')} • {allPosts.length} {t('parts')}
   103→          </span>
   104→          <h1 className="page-title mb-4">
   105→            {title}
   106→          </h1>
   107→          {description && (
   108→            <p className="text-lg text-muted font-serif italic leading-relaxed">
   109→              {description}
   110→            </p>
   111→          )}
   112→          {allPosts.length > 0 && (() => {
   113→            // Pick the first installment respecting sort order:
   114→            // date-desc (default) → oldest is last; date-asc/manual → oldest/first is [0]
   115→            const firstPost = (seriesData?.sort === 'date-asc' || seriesData?.sort === 'manual')
   116→              ? allPosts[0]
   117→              : allPosts[allPosts.length - 1];
   118→            return (
   119→            <Link
   120→              href={getPostUrl(firstPost)}
   121→              className="mt-6 inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-accent text-white text-sm font-medium hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors no-underline"
   122→            >
   123→              {t('start_reading')}
   124→              <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
   125→                <polyline points="9 18 15 12 9 6" />
   126→              </svg>
   127→            </Link>
   128→            );
   129→          })()}
   130→          {authors.length > 0 && (
   131→            <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted">
   132→              <span className="mr-1">{t('written_by')}</span>
   133→              {authors.map((author, index) => (
   134→                <span key={author}>
   135→                  <Link
   136→                    href={`/authors/${getAuthorSlug(author)}`}
   137→                    className="text-foreground hover:text-accent no-underline transition-colors duration-200"
   138→                  >
   139→                    {author}
   140→                  </Link>
   141→                  {index < authors.length - 1 && <span className="mr-1">,</span>}
   142→                </span>
   143→              ))}
   144→            </p>
   145→          )}
   146→        </div>
   147→      </header>
   148→
   149→      {/* Series Catalog */}
   150→      <SeriesCatalog posts={posts} totalPosts={allPosts.length} />
   151→
   152→      {totalPages > 1 && (
   153→        <div className="mt-12">
   154→          <Pagination currentPage={page} totalPages={totalPages} basePath={`/series/${slug}`} />
   155→        </div>
   156→      )}
   157→    </div>
   158→  );
   159→}
   160→

     1→'use client';

     3→import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

     9→
    10→interface SeriesSidebarProps {
    11→  seriesSlug: string;
    12→  seriesTitle: string;
    13→  posts: PostData[];
    14→  currentSlug: string;

    16→
    17→export default function SeriesSidebar({ seriesSlug, seriesTitle, posts, currentSlug }: SeriesSidebarProps) {
    18→  const { t } = useLanguage();
    19→  const currentIndex = posts.findIndex(p => p.slug === currentSlug);
    20→  const currentItemRef = useRef<HTMLLIElement>(null);
    21→  const sidebarRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
    22→
    23→  useEffect(() => {
    24→    if (currentItemRef.current && sidebarRef.current) {
    25→      const sidebar = sidebarRef.current;
    26→      const item = currentItemRef.current;
    27→      const itemTop = item.offsetTop;
    28→      const itemHeight = item.offsetHeight;
    29→      const sidebarHeight = sidebar.clientHeight;
    30→
    31→      // Scroll so the current item is roughly centered in the sidebar
    32→      sidebar.scrollTop = itemTop - sidebarHeight / 2 + itemHeight / 2;
    33→    }
    34→  }, [currentSlug]);
    35→
    36→  return (
    37→    <aside
    38→      ref={sidebarRef}
    39→      className="hidden lg:block sticky top-28 self-start max-h-[calc(100vh-8rem)] overflow-y-auto w-56 pr-4 scrollbar-hide"
    40→    >
    41→      {/* Series Header */}
    42→      <div className="mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-muted/10">
    43→        <Link
    44→          href={`/series/${seriesSlug}`}
    45→          className="group block no-underline"
    46→        >
    47→          <span className="text-[10px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-accent mb-2 block">
    48→            {t('series')}
    49→          </span>
    50→          <h3 className="font-serif font-bold text-heading text-lg leading-snug group-hover:text-accent transition-colors">
    51→            {seriesTitle}
    52→          </h3>
    53→        </Link>
    54→
    55→        {/* Progress indicator */}
    56→        <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-3">
    57→          <div className="flex-1 h-1 bg-muted/10 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
    58→            <div
    59→              className="h-full bg-accent/60 rounded-full transition-all duration-500"
    60→              style={{ width: `${((currentIndex + 1) / posts.length) * 100}%` }}
    61→            />
    62→          </div>
    63→          <span className="text-xs font-mono text-muted whitespace-nowrap">
    64→            {currentIndex + 1}/{posts.length}
    65→          </span>
    66→        </div>
    67→      </div>
    68→
    69→      {/* Posts List */}
    70→      <nav aria-label="Series navigation">
    71→        <ul className="space-y-1 relative">
    72→          {/* Timeline connector line */}
    73→          <div className="absolute left-[11px] top-3 bottom-3 w-px bg-muted/15" />
    74→
    75→          {posts.map((post, index) => {
    76→            const isCurrent = post.slug === currentSlug;
    77→            const isPast = index < currentIndex;
    78→
    79→            return (
    80→              <li key={post.slug} ref={isCurrent ? currentItemRef : undefined} className="relative">
    81→                <Link
    82→                  href={getPostUrl(post)}
    83→                  className={`group flex items-start gap-3 py-2 px-2 -mx-2 rounded-lg no-underline transition-all duration-200 ${
    84→                    isCurrent
    85→                      ? 'bg-accent/5'
    86→                      : 'hover:bg-muted/5'
    87→                  }`}
    88→                  aria-current={isCurrent ? 'page' : undefined}
    89→                >
    90→                  {/* Number indicator */}
    91→                  <div className={`relative z-10 flex-shrink-0 w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-mono font-bold transition-colors ${
    92→                    isCurrent
    93→                      ? 'bg-accent text-white shadow-sm shadow-accent/30'
    94→                      : isPast
    95→                        ? 'bg-accent/20 text-accent'
    96→                        : 'bg-muted/10 text-muted group-hover:bg-muted/20 group-hover:text-foreground'
    97→                  }`}>
    98→                    {padNumber(index + 1)}
    99→                  </div>
   100→
   101→                  {/* Content */}
   102→                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 pt-0.5">
   103→                    <span className={`block text-sm leading-snug transition-colors ${
   104→                      isCurrent
   105→                        ? 'text-accent font-semibold'
   106→                        : isPast
   107→                          ? 'text-foreground/70 group-hover:text-foreground'
   108→                          : 'text-muted group-hover:text-foreground'
   109→                    }`}>
   110→                      {post.title}
   111→                    </span>
   112→                  </div>
   113→                </Link>
   114→              </li>
   115→            );
   116→          })}
   117→        </ul>
   118→      </nav>
   119→
   120→      {/* Footer link */}
   121→      <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-muted/10">
   122→        <Link
   123→          href={`/series/${seriesSlug}`}
   124→          className="text-xs font-sans text-muted hover:text-accent transition-colors no-underline flex items-center gap-1"
   125→        >
   126→          {t('view_full_series')}
   127→          <svg className="w-3 h-3" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}>
   128→            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M9 5l7 7-7 7" />
   129→          </svg>
   130→        </Link>
   131→      </div>
   132→    </aside>
   133→  );
   134→}

    18→export default function SeriesList({ seriesSlug, seriesTitle, posts, currentSlug }: SeriesListProps) {
    19→  const { t } = useLanguage();
    20→  const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(false);
    21→
    22→  if (!posts || posts.length === 0) return null;
    23→
    24→  const currentIndex = posts.findIndex(p => p.slug === currentSlug);
    25→  const prevPost = currentIndex > 0 ? posts[currentIndex - 1] : null;
    26→  const nextPost = currentIndex < posts.length - 1 ? posts[currentIndex + 1] : null;
    27→
    28→  return (
    29→    <div data-testid="series-list" className="p-5 bg-muted/5 rounded-xl border border-muted/20">
    30→      {/* Header */}
    31→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
    32→        <Link
    33→          href={`/series/${seriesSlug}`}
    34→          className="group flex items-center gap-2 no-underline"
    35→        >
    36→          <span className="text-[10px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-accent">
    37→            {t('series')}
    38→          </span>
    39→          <span className="text-[10px] text-muted">•</span>
    40→          <span className="text-sm font-serif font-bold text-heading group-hover:text-accent transition-colors">
    41→            {seriesTitle}
    42→          </span>
    43→        </Link>
    44→        <span className="text-xs font-mono text-muted bg-muted/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
    45→          {currentIndex + 1}/{posts.length}
    46→        </span>
    47→      </div>
    48→
    49→      {/* Progress bar */}
    50→      <div className="h-1 bg-muted/10 rounded-full overflow-hidden mb-4">
    51→        <div
    52→          className="h-full bg-accent/60 rounded-full transition-all duration-500"
    53→          style={{ width: `${((currentIndex + 1) / posts.length) * 100}%` }}
    54→        />
    55→      </div>
    56→
    57→      {/* Prev / Next navigation */}
    58→      <div className="mb-3">
    59→        <PrevNextNav
    60→          prev={prevPost ? { href: getPostUrl(prevPost), title: prevPost.title } : null}
    61→          next={nextPost ? { href: getPostUrl(nextPost), title: nextPost.title } : null}
    62→        />
    63→      </div>
    64→
    65→      {/* Toggle to expand full list */}
    66→      <button
    67→        onClick={() => setIsExpanded(!isExpanded)}
    68→        className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2 text-xs font-sans text-muted hover:text-accent transition-colors"
    69→      >
    70→        <span className="h-px flex-1 bg-muted/10" />
    71→        <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
    72→          {isExpanded ? t('hide') : t('all_posts')}
    73→          <svg

    75→            fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}
    76→          >

    78→          </svg>
    79→        </span>
    80→        <span className="h-px flex-1 bg-muted/10" />
    81→      </button>
    82→
    83→      {/* Collapsible posts list */}
    84→      {isExpanded && (
    85→        <ol className="space-y-2 mt-3 animate-slide-down">
    86→          {posts.map((post, index) => {
    87→            const isCurrent = post.slug === currentSlug;

    89→

    96→                    </span>

    98→                      {post.title}
    99→                    </span>
   100→                  </div>

     1→import { getPostBySlug, getAllPosts, getRelatedPosts, getSeriesPosts, getSeriesData, getAdjacentPosts, buildSlugRegistry, getBacklinks, PostData } from '@/lib/markdown';
     2→import { notFound } from 'next/navigation';
     3→import PostLayout from '@/layouts/PostLayout';
     4→import SimpleLayout from '@/layouts/SimpleLayout';
     5→import { Metadata } from 'next';
     6→import { siteConfig } from '../../../../site.config';
     7→import { resolveLocale } from '@/lib/i18n';
     8→import { getPostsBasePath, getPostUrl } from '@/lib/urls';
     9→import { buildPostJsonLd, serializeJsonLd, resolveImageUrl } from '@/lib/json-ld';
    10→
    11→function safeDecodeParam(param: string): string {
    12→  try {
    13→    return decodeURIComponent(param);
    14→  } catch {
    15→    return param;
    16→  }
    17→}
    18→
    19→function resolvePostFromParam(rawSlug: string) {
    20→  const decoded = safeDecodeParam(rawSlug);
    21→  return (
    22→    getPostBySlug(decoded) ||
    23→    getPostBySlug(rawSlug) ||
    24→    getPostBySlug(decoded.normalize('NFC')) ||
    25→    getPostBySlug(decoded.normalize('NFD'))
    26→  );
    27→}
    28→
    29→/**
    30→ * Generates the static paths for all blog posts at build time.
    31→ * This ensures fast page loads and SEO optimization.
    32→ */
    33→export async function generateStaticParams() {
    34→  if (getPostsBasePath() !== 'posts') return [{ slug: '_' }]; // Route disabled; custom path handles this
    35→  const posts = getAllPosts();
    36→  if (posts.length === 0) return [{ slug: '_' }];
    37→  // Work around Next dev static-param checks for percent-encoded Unicode paths
    38→  // under `output: "export"` by including encoded variants only in development.
    39→  // Production export keeps raw segment values.
    40→  const slugs = new Set<string>();
    41→  for (const post of posts) {
    42→    slugs.add(post.slug);
    43→    if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {
    44→      slugs.add(encodeURIComponent(post.slug));
    45→    }
    46→  }
    47→  return Array.from(slugs).map((slug) => ({ slug }));
    48→}
    49→
    50→export const dynamicParams = false;
    51→
    52→export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }): Promise<Metadata> {
    53→  const { slug: rawSlug } = await params;
    54→  const post = resolvePostFromParam(rawSlug);
    55→
    56→  if (!post) {
    57→    return {
    58→      title: 'Post Not Found',
    59→    };
    60→  }
    61→
    62→  const siteUrl = siteConfig.baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '');
    63→  const ogImage = resolveImageUrl(post.coverImage, siteConfig.ogImage, siteUrl);
    64→
    65→  return {
    66→    title: `${post.title} | ${resolveLocale(siteConfig.title)}`,
    67→    description: post.excerpt,
    68→    openGraph: {
    69→      title: post.title,
    70→      description: post.excerpt,
    71→      type: 'article',
    72→      publishedTime: post.date,
    73→      authors: post.authors,
    74→      images: [
    75→        {
    76→          url: ogImage,
    77→          width: 1200,
    78→          height: 630,
    79→          alt: post.title,
    80→        },
    81→      ],
    82→      siteName: resolveLocale(siteConfig.title),
    83→    },
    84→    twitter: {
    85→      card: 'summary_large_image',
    86→      title: post.title,
    87→      description: post.excerpt,
    88→      images: [ogImage],
    89→    },
    90→  };
    91→}
    92→
    93→export default async function PostPage({
    94→  params,
    95→}: {
    96→  params: Promise<{ slug: string }>;
    97→}) {
    98→  const { slug: rawSlug } = await params;
    99→  const slug = safeDecodeParam(rawSlug);
   100→  const post = resolvePostFromParam(rawSlug);
   101→
   102→  if (!post) {
   103→    notFound();
   104→  }
   105→
   106→  // Determine layout based on frontmatter
   107→  const layout = post.layout || 'post';
   108→
   109→  const siteUrl = siteConfig.baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '');
   110→  const jsonLd = buildPostJsonLd({
   111→    post,
   112→    postUrl: `${siteUrl}${getPostUrl(post)}`,
   113→    siteTitle: resolveLocale(siteConfig.title),
   114→    siteUrl,
   115→    defaultOgImage: siteConfig.ogImage,
   116→  });
   117→  const jsonLdScript = <script type="application/ld+json" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: serializeJsonLd(jsonLd) }} />;
   118→
   119→  if (layout === 'simple') {
   120→    return <>{jsonLdScript}<SimpleLayout post={post} /></>;
   121→  }
   122→
   123→  const relatedPosts = getRelatedPosts(slug);
   124→  const { prev, next } = getAdjacentPosts(slug);
   125→  const slugRegistry = buildSlugRegistry();
   126→  const backlinks = getBacklinks(slug);
   127→  let seriesPosts: PostData[] = [];
   128→  let seriesTitle: string | undefined;
   129→
   130→  if (post.series) {
   131→    seriesPosts = getSeriesPosts(post.series);
   132→    const seriesData = getSeriesData(post.series);
   133→    seriesTitle = seriesData?.title;
   134→  }
   135→
   136→  return (
   137→    <>
   138→      {jsonLdScript}
   139→      <PostLayout post={post} relatedPosts={relatedPosts} seriesPosts={seriesPosts} seriesTitle={seriesTitle} prevPost={prev} nextPost={next} backlinks={backlinks} slugRegistry={slugRegistry} />
   140→    </>
   141→  );
   142→}

     1→'use client';

     3→import { useState, useRef } from 'react';

    10→import TocPanel from './TocPanel';

    12→import { siteConfig } from '../../site.config';
    13→
    14→interface PostSidebarProps {
    15→  seriesSlug?: string;
    16→  seriesTitle?: string;
    17→  posts?: PostData[];
    18→  currentSlug: string;
    19→  headings: Heading[];
    20→  localeHeadings?: Record<string, Heading[]>;
    21→  shareUrl?: string;
    22→  shareTitle?: string;
    23→}
    24→
    25→function getVisibleIndices(total: number, current: number): (number | 'ellipsis')[] {
    26→  if (total <= 7) return Array.from({ length: total }, (_, i) => i);
    27→  const result: (number | 'ellipsis')[] = [];
    28→  result.push(0);
    29→  const windowStart = Math.max(1, current - 2);
    30→  const windowEnd = Math.min(total - 2, current + 2);
    31→  if (windowStart > 1) result.push('ellipsis');
    32→  for (let i = windowStart; i <= windowEnd; i++) result.push(i);
    33→  if (windowEnd < total - 2) result.push('ellipsis');
    34→  result.push(total - 1);
    35→  return result;
    36→}
    37→
    38→export default function PostSidebar({ seriesSlug, seriesTitle, posts, currentSlug, headings, localeHeadings, shareUrl, shareTitle }: PostSidebarProps) {
    39→  const { t, language } = useLanguage();
    40→  const activeHeadings = localeHeadings?.[language] ?? headings;
    41→  const hasSeries = !!(seriesSlug && posts && posts.length > 0);
    42→  const currentIndex = hasSeries ? posts!.findIndex(p => p.slug === currentSlug) : -1;
    43→  // Chronological sort (ascending date) — used for both progress counter and isPast styling
    44→  const sortedPosts = hasSeries
    45→    ? [...posts!].sort((a, b) => new Date(a.date).getTime() - new Date(b.date).getTime())
    46→    : null;
    47→  const progressIndex = hasSeries ? sortedPosts!.findIndex(p => p.slug === currentSlug) : -1;
    48→  const currentItemRef = useRef<HTMLLIElement>(null);
    49→  const sidebarRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
    50→  const [seriesCollapsed, setSeriesCollapsed] = useState(false);
    51→  useSidebarAutoScroll(sidebarRef, currentItemRef, currentSlug);
    52→
    53→  return (
    54→    <aside
    55→      ref={sidebarRef}
    56→      data-testid="post-sidebar"
    57→      className="hidden lg:block sticky top-20 self-start w-[280px] max-h-[calc(100vh-6rem)] overflow-y-auto pr-4 scrollbar-hide hover:scrollbar-thin"
    58→    >
    59→      {/* TOC — always at top */}
    60→      <TocPanel
    61→        headings={activeHeadings}
    62→        className={`mb-6 ${hasSeries ? 'pb-4 border-b border-muted/10' : ''}`}
    63→      />
    64→
    65→      {/* Series section — below TOC */}
    66→      {hasSeries && (
    67→        <div>
    68→          {/* Header — always visible */}
    69→          <div className="mb-3">
    70→            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
    71→              <span className="text-[10px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-accent">
    72→                {t('series')}
    73→              </span>
    74→              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-muted/60">
    75→                {progressIndex >= 0 ? progressIndex + 1 : '?'} / {posts!.length}
    76→              </span>
    77→            </div>
    78→            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
    79→              <Link href={`/series/${seriesSlug}`} className="group block no-underline flex-1 min-w-0">
    80→                <h3 className="font-serif font-bold text-heading text-base leading-snug group-hover:text-accent transition-colors">
    81→                  {seriesTitle}
    82→                </h3>
    83→              </Link>
    84→              <button
    85→                onClick={() => setSeriesCollapsed(prev => !prev)}
    86→                className="flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-muted hover:text-foreground transition-colors"
    87→                aria-label={seriesCollapsed ? 'Expand series' : 'Collapse series'}
    88→              >
    89→                <svg
    90→                  className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform duration-200 ${seriesCollapsed ? '' : 'rotate-180'}`}
    91→                  fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}
    92→                >
    93→                  <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7" />
    94→                </svg>
    95→              </button>
    96→            </div>
    97→          </div>
    98→
    99→          {/* Collapsible: post list + footer link */}
   100→          {!seriesCollapsed && (
   101→            <>
   102→              <nav aria-label="Series navigation" className="mb-4 animate-slide-down">
   103→                <ul className="space-y-1 relative before:absolute before:left-[11px] before:top-3 before:bottom-3 before:w-px before:bg-muted/15">
   104→                  {getVisibleIndices(posts!.length, currentIndex).map((item, i) => {

     2→import { notFound } from 'next/navigation';

    10→
    11→function safeDecodeParam(param: string): string {
    12→  try {
    13→    return decodeURIComponent(param);
    14→  } catch {
    15→    return param;
    16→  }
    17→}
    18→
    19→function resolvePostFromParam(rawSlug: string) {
    20→  const decoded = safeDecodeParam(rawSlug);
    21→  return (
    22→    getPostBySlug(decoded) ||
    23→    getPostBySlug(rawSlug) ||
    24→    getPostBySlug(decoded.normalize('NFC')) ||
    25→    getPostBySlug(decoded.normalize('NFD'))
    26→  );
    27→}
    28→
    29→export async function generateStaticParams() {
    30→  const params: { slug: string; postSlug: string }[] = [];
    31→
    32→  // Custom posts basePath — all posts served at /[basePath]/[slug]
    33→  const basePath = getPostsBasePath();
    34→  if (basePath !== 'posts') {
    35→    getAllPosts().forEach(post => { params.push({ slug: basePath, postSlug: post.slug }); });
    36→  }
    37→
    38→  // Series custom paths — only posts belonging to that series
    39→  for (const [seriesSlug, customPath] of Object.entries(getSeriesCustomPaths())) {
    40→    getSeriesPosts(seriesSlug).forEach(post => { params.push({ slug: customPath, postSlug: post.slug }); });
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  // Placeholder keeps Next.js happy with output: export when no custom paths configured.
    44→  // dynamicParams = false ensures any unrecognised slug/postSlug combo returns 404.
    45→  return params.length > 0 ? params : [{ slug: '_', postSlug: '_' }];
    46→}
    47→
    48→export const dynamicParams = false;
    49→
    50→export async function generateMetadata({
    51→  params,
    52→}: {
    53→  params: Promise<{ slug: string; postSlug: string }>;
    54→}): Promise<Metadata> {
    55→  const { postSlug: rawPostSlug } = await params;
    56→  const post = resolvePostFromParam(rawPostSlug);
    57→
    58→  if (!post) {
    59→    return { title: 'Post Not Found' };
    60→  }
    61→
    62→  const ogImage =
    63→    post.coverImage && !post.coverImage.startsWith('text:') && !post.coverImage.startsWith('./')
    64→      ? post.coverImage
    65→      : siteConfig.ogImage;
    66→
    67→  return {
    68→    title: `${post.title} | ${resolveLocale(siteConfig.title)}`,
    69→    description: post.excerpt,
    70→    openGraph: {
    71→      title: post.title,
    72→      description: post.excerpt,
    73→      type: 'article',
    74→      publishedTime: post.date,
    75→      authors: post.authors,
    76→      images: [
    77→        {
    78→          url: ogImage,
    79→          width: 1200,
    80→          height: 630,
    81→          alt: post.title,
    82→        },
    83→      ],
    84→      siteName: resolveLocale(siteConfig.title),
    85→    },
    86→    twitter: {
    87→      card: 'summary_large_image',
    88→      title: post.title,
    89→      description: post.excerpt,
    90→      images: [ogImage],
    91→    },
    92→  };
    93→}
    94→
    95→export default async function PrefixPostPage({
    96→  params,
    97→}: {
    98→  params: Promise<{ slug: string; postSlug: string }>;
    99→}) {
   100→  const { slug: prefix, postSlug: rawPostSlug } = await params;
   101→
   102→  // Validate the prefix is a known custom path
   103→  const basePath = getPostsBasePath();
   104→  const customPaths = getSeriesCustomPaths();
   105→  const isValidBasePath = prefix === basePath && basePath !== 'posts';
   106→  const matchedSeriesSlug = Object.entries(customPaths).find(([, path]) => path === prefix)?.[0];
   107→
   108→  if (!isValidBasePath && !matchedSeriesSlug) {
   109→    notFound();
   110→  }
   111→
   112→  const post = resolvePostFromParam(rawPostSlug);
   113→  if (!post) {
   114→    notFound();
   115→  }
   116→
   117→  const layout = post.layout || 'post';
   118→
   119→  const siteUrl = siteConfig.baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '');
   120→  const jsonLd = buildPostJsonLd({
   121→    post,
   122→    postUrl: `${siteUrl}${getPostUrl(post)}`,
   123→    siteTitle: resolveLocale(siteConfig.title),
   124→    siteUrl,
   125→    defaultOgImage: siteConfig.ogImage,
   126→  });
   127→  const jsonLdScript = <script type="application/ld+json" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: serializeJsonLd(jsonLd) }} />;
   128→
   129→  if (layout === 'simple') {
   130→    return <>{jsonLdScript}<SimpleLayout post={post} /></>;
   131→  }
   132→
   133→  const relatedPosts = getRelatedPosts(post.slug);
   134→  const { prev, next } = getAdjacentPosts(post.slug);
   135→  const slugRegistry = buildSlugRegistry();
   136→  const backlinks = getBacklinks(post.slug);
   137→  let seriesPosts: PostData[] = [];
   138→  let seriesTitle: string | undefined;
   139→
   140→  if (post.series) {
   141→    seriesPosts = getSeriesPosts(post.series);
   142→    const seriesData = getSeriesData(post.series);
   143→    seriesTitle = seriesData?.title;
   144→  }
   145→
   146→  return (
   147→    <>
   148→      {jsonLdScript}
   149→      <PostLayout
   150→        post={post}
   151→        relatedPosts={relatedPosts}
   152→        seriesPosts={seriesPosts}
   153→        seriesTitle={seriesTitle}
   154→        prevPost={prev}
   155→        nextPost={next}
   156→        backlinks={backlinks}
   157→        slugRegistry={slugRegistry}
   158→      />
   159→    </>
   160→  );
   161→}

   788→export function getAdjacentPosts(slug: string): { prev: PostData | null; next: PostData | null } {
   789→  const allPosts = getAllPosts(); // sorted desc by date (newest first)
   790→  const index = allPosts.findIndex(p => p.slug === slug);
   791→  if (index === -1) return { prev: null, next: null };
   792→  return {
   793→    prev: index < allPosts.length - 1 ? allPosts[index + 1] : null, // older post
   794→    next: index > 0 ? allPosts[index - 1] : null,                   // newer post
   795→  };
   796→}
   797→
   798→export function getFeaturedSeries(): Record<string, PostData[]> {
   799→  const allSeries = getAllSeries();
   800→  const featuredSeries: Record<string, PostData[]> = {};
   801→  
   802→  Object.keys(allSeries).forEach(slug => {
   803→    const seriesData = getSeriesData(slug);
   804→    if (seriesData?.featured) {
   805→      featuredSeries[slug] = allSeries[slug];
   806→    }
   807→  });
   808→  
   809→  return featuredSeries;
   810→}

     1→import { siteConfig } from '../../site.config';
     2→
     3→/** Strip leading and trailing slashes so path segments compose cleanly. */
     4→function normalizeSegment(segment: string): string {
     5→  return segment.replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, '');
     6→}
     7→
     8→export function getPostsBasePath(): string {
     9→  return normalizeSegment(siteConfig.posts?.basePath ?? 'posts') || 'posts';
    10→}
    11→
    12→export function getSeriesCustomPaths(): Record<string, string> {
    13→  const raw = siteConfig.series?.customPaths ?? {};
    14→  return Object.fromEntries(
    15→    Object.entries(raw).map(([k, v]) => [k, normalizeSegment(v)])
    16→  );
    17→}
    18→
    19→/** Returns the canonical URL path for a post, respecting series custom paths and posts basePath. */
    20→export function getPostUrl(post: { slug: string; series?: string }): string {
    21→  if (post.series) {
    22→    const customPath = getSeriesCustomPaths()[post.series];
    23→    if (customPath) return `/${customPath}/${post.slug}`;
    24→  }
    25→  return `/${getPostsBasePath()}/${post.slug}`;
    26→}
    27→
    28→/** Returns the posts listing URL (page 1). */
    29→export function getPostsListUrl(): string {
    30→  return `/${getPostsBasePath()}`;
    31→}
    32→
    33→/** Returns a posts listing page URL (page 2+). */
    34→export function getPostsPageUrl(page: number): string {
    35→  return `/${getPostsBasePath()}/page/${page}`;
    36→}

    39→export function getBooksListUrl(): string {
    40→  return '/books';

    49→export function getBookChapterUrl(bookSlug: string, chapterSlug: string): string {

    51→}
    52→
    53→/** Returns the canonical URL path for a flow note. */

  "old_string": "export function getSeriesData(slug: string): PostData | null {\n  if (!fs.existsSync(seriesDirectory)) return null;\n  const indexPathMdx = path.join(seriesDirectory, slug, 'index.mdx');\n  const indexPathMd = path.join(seriesDirectory, slug, 'index.md');\n  \n  let fullPath = '';\n  if (fs.existsSync(indexPathMdx)) fullPath = indexPathMdx;\n  else if (fs.existsSync(indexPathMd)) fullPath = indexPathMd;\n  else return null;\n\n  return parseMarkdownFile(fullPath, slug, undefined, slug);\n}\n\n// ─── Books ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────",
  "new_string": "export function getSeriesData(slug: string): PostData | null {\n  if (!fs.existsSync(seriesDirectory)) return null;\n  const indexPathMdx = path.join(seriesDirectory, slug, 'index.mdx');\n  const indexPathMd = path.join(seriesDirectory, slug, 'index.md');\n\n  let fullPath = '';\n  if (fs.existsSync(indexPathMdx)) fullPath = indexPathMdx;\n  else if (fs.existsSync(indexPathMd)) fullPath = indexPathMd;\n  else return null;\n\n  return parseMarkdownFile(fullPath, slug, undefined, slug);\n}\n\nexport function getCollectionPosts(collectionSlug: string): PostData[] {\n  const data = getSeriesData(collectionSlug);\n  if (data?.type !== 'collection' || !data.items) return [];\n\n  return data.items.flatMap(item => {\n    if ('series' in item) {\n      const posts = getSeriesPosts(item.series);\n      return item.exclude ? posts.filter(p => !item.exclude!.includes(p.slug)) : posts;\n    }\n    const post = getPostBySlug(item.post);\n    return post ? [post] : [];\n  });\n}\n\nexport function getCollectionsForPost(postSlug: string): CollectionContext[] {\n  if (!fs.existsSync(seriesDirectory)) return [];\n  const seriesFolders = fs.readdirSync(seriesDirectory, { withFileTypes: true });\n  const results: CollectionContext[] = [];\n\n  for (const folder of seriesFolders) {\n    if (!folder.isDirectory()) continue;\n    const data = getSeriesData(folder.name);\n    if (data?.type !== 'collection') continue;\n    const posts = getCollectionPosts(folder.name);\n    if (posts.some(p => p.slug === postSlug)) {\n      results.push({ slug: folder.name, title: data.title, posts });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return results;\n}\n\n// ─── Books ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────"
}
```

  "old_string": "/** Returns the canonical URL path for a static page (e.g. /about, /subscribe). */\nexport function getStaticPageUrl(slug: string): string {\n  return `/${slug}`;\n}",
  "new_string": "/** Returns the canonical URL path for a static page (e.g. /about, /subscribe). */\nexport function getStaticPageUrl(slug: string): string {\n  return `/${slug}`;\n}\n\n/** Returns the post URL with a ?collection= query param to preserve collection navigation context. */\nexport function getPostUrlInCollection(post: { slug: string; series?: string }, collectionSlug: string): string {\n  return `${getPostUrl(post)}?collection=${collectionSlug}`;\n}"
}

  "old_string": "export default function PostSidebar({ seriesSlug, seriesTitle, posts, currentSlug, headings, localeHeadings, shareUrl, shareTitle }: PostSidebarProps) {\n  const { t, language } = useLanguage();\n  const activeHeadings = localeHeadings?.[language] ?? headings;\n  const hasSeries = !!(seriesSlug && posts && posts.length > 0);\n  const currentIndex = hasSeries ? posts!.findIndex(p => p.slug === currentSlug) : -1;\n  // Chronological sort (ascending date) — used for both progress counter and isPast styling\n  const sortedPosts = hasSeries\n    ? [...posts!].sort((a, b) => new Date(a.date).getTime() - new Date(b.date).getTime())\n    : null;\n  const progressIndex = hasSeries ? sortedPosts!.findIndex(p => p.slug === currentSlug) : -1;\n  const currentItemRef = useRef<HTMLLIElement>(null);\n  const sidebarRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);\n  const [seriesCollapsed, setSeriesCollapsed] = useState(false);\n  useSidebarAutoScroll(sidebarRef, currentItemRef, currentSlug);",
  "new_string": "export default function PostSidebar({ seriesSlug, seriesTitle, posts, collectionContexts, currentSlug, headings, localeHeadings, shareUrl, shareTitle }: PostSidebarProps) {\n  const { t, language } = useLanguage();\n  const searchParams = useSearchParams();\n  const collectionParam = searchParams.get('collection');\n  const activeCollection = collectionParam\n    ? (collectionContexts ?? []).find(c => c.slug === collectionParam) ?? null\n    : null;\n\n  const effectiveSlug = activeCollection?.slug ?? seriesSlug;\n  const effectiveTitle = activeCollection?.title ?? seriesTitle;\n  const effectivePosts = activeCollection?.posts ?? posts;\n  const isCollectionContext = !!activeCollection;\n\n  const postHref = (post: PostData) =>\n    isCollectionContext ? getPostUrlInCollection(post, activeCollection!.slug) : getPostUrl(post);\n\n  const activeHeadings = localeHeadings?.[language] ?? headings;\n  const hasSeries = !!(effectiveSlug && effectivePosts && effectivePosts.length > 0);\n  const currentIndex = hasSeries ? effectivePosts!.findIndex(p => p.slug === currentSlug) : -1;\n  // Chronological sort (ascending date) — used for progress counter and isPast styling in series mode.\n  // In collection mode, use the collection's defined order directly.\n  const sortedPosts = hasSeries && !isCollectionContext\n    ? [...effectivePosts!].sort((a, b) => new Date(a.date).getTime() - new Date(b.date).getTime())\n    : null;\n  const progressIndex = hasSeries\n    ? (sortedPosts ? sortedPosts.findIndex(p => p.slug === currentSlug) : currentIndex)\n    : -1;\n  const currentItemRef = useRef<HTMLLIElement>(null);\n  const sidebarRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);\n  const [seriesCollapsed, setSeriesCollapsed] = useState(false);\n  useSidebarAutoScroll(sidebarRef, currentItemRef, currentSlug);"
}

  "old_string": "'use client';\n\nimport { useState } from 'react';\nimport Link from 'next/link';\nimport { PostData } from '@/lib/markdown';\nimport { useLanguage } from './LanguageProvider';\nimport { getPostUrl } from '@/lib/urls';\nimport PrevNextNav from './PrevNextNav';\nimport { padNumber } from '@/lib/format-utils';\n\ninterface SeriesListProps {\n  seriesSlug: string;\n  seriesTitle: string;\n  posts: PostData[];\n  currentSlug: string;\n}\n\nexport default function SeriesList({ seriesSlug, seriesTitle, posts, currentSlug }: SeriesListProps) {\n  const { t } = useLanguage();\n  const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(false);\n\n  if (!posts || posts.length === 0) return null;\n\n  const currentIndex = posts.findIndex(p => p.slug === currentSlug);\n  const prevPost = currentIndex > 0 ? posts[currentIndex - 1] : null;\n  const nextPost = currentIndex < posts.length - 1 ? posts[currentIndex + 1] : null;",
  "new_string": "'use client';\n\nimport { useState } from 'react';\nimport { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';\nimport Link from 'next/link';\nimport { PostData, CollectionContext } from '@/lib/markdown';\nimport { useLanguage } from './LanguageProvider';\nimport { getPostUrl, getPostUrlInCollection } from '@/lib/urls';\nimport PrevNextNav from './PrevNextNav';\nimport { padNumber } from '@/lib/format-utils';\n\ninterface SeriesListProps {\n  seriesSlug?: string;\n  seriesTitle?: string;\n  posts?: PostData[];\n  collectionContexts?: CollectionContext[];\n  currentSlug: string;\n}\n\nexport default function SeriesList({ seriesSlug, seriesTitle, posts, collectionContexts, currentSlug }: SeriesListProps) {\n  const { t } = useLanguage();\n  const searchParams = useSearchParams();\n  const collectionParam = searchParams.get('collection');\n  const activeCollection = collectionParam\n    ? (collectionContexts ?? []).find(c => c.slug === collectionParam) ?? null\n    : null;\n\n  const effectiveSlug = activeCollection?.slug ?? seriesSlug;\n  const effectiveTitle = activeCollection?.title ?? seriesTitle;\n  const effectivePosts = activeCollection?.posts ?? posts;\n  const isCollectionContext = !!activeCollection;\n\n  const postHref = (post: PostData) =>\n    isCollectionContext ? getPostUrlInCollection(post, activeCollection!.slug) : getPostUrl(post);\n\n  const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(false);\n\n  if (!effectivePosts || effectivePosts.length === 0) return null;\n\n  const currentIndex = effectivePosts.findIndex(p => p.slug === currentSlug);\n  const prevPost = currentIndex > 0 ? effectivePosts[currentIndex - 1] : null;\n  const nextPost = currentIndex < effectivePosts.length - 1 ? effectivePosts[currentIndex + 1] : null;"
}

}

     1→import { getSeriesData, getSeriesPosts, getAllSeries, resolveSeriesAuthors, getAuthorSlug } from '@/lib/markdown';
     2→import { notFound } from 'next/navigation';
     3→import SeriesCatalog from '@/components/SeriesCatalog';
     4→import Pagination from '@/components/Pagination';
     5→import { Metadata } from 'next';
     6→import { siteConfig } from '../../../../../../site.config';
     7→import CoverImage from '@/components/CoverImage';
     8→import Link from 'next/link';
     9→import { t, resolveLocale, tWith } from '@/lib/i18n';
    10→
    11→const PAGE_SIZE = siteConfig.pagination.series;
    12→
    13→export async function generateStaticParams() {
    14→  const allSeries = getAllSeries();
    15→  const params: { slug: string; page: string }[] = [];
    16→  
    17→  Object.keys(allSeries).forEach(slug => {
    18→    const posts = allSeries[slug];
    19→    const totalPages = Math.ceil(posts.length / PAGE_SIZE);
    20→    if (totalPages > 1) {
    21→        for (let i = 2; i <= totalPages; i++) {
    22→            params.push({ slug, page: i.toString() });
    23→        }
    24→    }
    25→  });
    26→  if (params.length === 0) return [{ slug: '_', page: '2' }];
    27→  return params;
    28→}
    29→
    30→export const dynamicParams = false;
    31→
    32→export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string; page: string }> }): Promise<Metadata> {
    33→  const { slug: rawSlug, page } = await params;
    34→  const slug = decodeURIComponent(rawSlug);
    35→  const seriesData = getSeriesData(slug);
    36→  const title = seriesData?.title || slug;
    37→  const allPosts = getSeriesPosts(slug);
    38→  const totalPages = Math.ceil(allPosts.length / PAGE_SIZE);
    39→  return {
    40→    title: `${title} - ${tWith('page_of_total', { page, total: totalPages })} | ${resolveLocale(siteConfig.title)}`,
    41→  };
    42→}
    43→
    44→export default async function SeriesPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string; page: string }> }) {
    45→  const { slug: rawSlug, page: pageStr } = await params;
    46→  const slug = decodeURIComponent(rawSlug);
    47→  const page = parseInt(pageStr);
    48→  const seriesData = getSeriesData(slug);
    49→  const allPosts = getSeriesPosts(slug);
    50→
    51→  if ((!seriesData && allPosts.length === 0) || (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' && seriesData?.draft)) {
    52→    notFound();
    53→  }
    54→
    55→  const totalPages = Math.ceil(allPosts.length / PAGE_SIZE);
    56→  
    57→  const start = (page - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;
    58→  const end = start + PAGE_SIZE;
    59→  const posts = allPosts.slice(start, end);
    60→
    61→  // Fallback title
    62→  const title = seriesData?.title || slug.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + slug.slice(1);
    63→  const description = seriesData?.excerpt;
    64→  const coverImage = seriesData?.coverImage;
    65→
    66→  const authors = resolveSeriesAuthors(slug, allPosts);
    67→
    68→  // Calculate the starting index for this page
    69→  const startIndex = (page - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;
    70→
    71→  return (
    72→    <div className="layout-main">
    73→      <header className="mb-16">
    74→        {/* Cover image hero */}
    75→        {coverImage && (
    76→          <div className="relative w-full h-56 md:h-72 mb-10 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-xl shadow-accent/5">
    77→            <CoverImage
    78→              src={coverImage}
    79→              title={title}
    80→              slug={slug}
    81→              className="w-full h-full object-cover"
    82→            />
    83→            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-background/60 to-transparent" />
    84→          </div>
    85→        )}
    86→
    87→        <div className="text-center max-w-2xl mx-auto">
    88→          <span className="badge-accent mb-4">
    89→            {t('series')} • {allPosts.length} {t('parts')}
    90→          </span>
    91→          <h1 className="page-title mb-2">{title}</h1>
    92→          <p className="text-base text-muted font-sans mt-1 mb-4">{tWith('page_of_total', { page, total: totalPages })}</p>
    93→          {description && (
    94→            <p className="text-lg text-muted font-serif italic leading-relaxed">
    95→              {description}
    96→            </p>
    97→          )}
    98→          {authors.length > 0 && (
    99→            <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted">
   100→              <span className="mr-1">{t('written_by')}</span>
   101→              {authors.map((author, index) => (
   102→                <span key={author}>
   103→                  <Link
   104→                    href={`/authors/${getAuthorSlug(author)}`}
   105→                    className="text-foreground hover:text-accent no-underline transition-colors duration-200"
   106→                  >
   107→                    {author}
   108→                  </Link>
   109→                  {index < authors.length - 1 && <span className="mr-1">,</span>}
   110→                </span>
   111→              ))}
   112→            </p>
   113→          )}
   114→        </div>
   115→      </header>
   116→
   117→      {/* Series Catalog */}
   118→      <SeriesCatalog posts={posts} startIndex={startIndex} totalPosts={allPosts.length} />
   119→
   120→      <div className="mt-12">
   121→        <Pagination currentPage={page} totalPages={totalPages} basePath={`/series/${slug}`} />
   122→      </div>
   123→    </div>
   124→  );
   125→}
   126→

> AGENT

All 129 tests pass. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

---

## What was built

### Data layer (`markdown.ts`)
- `CollectionItem` type — `{ series: string; exclude?: string[]; label?: string }` or `{ post: string; label?: string }`
- `CollectionContext` interface — `{ slug, title, posts }` for passing to client components
- `type` and `items` fields added to `PostSchema` and `PostData`
- `getCollectionPosts(slug)` — resolves a collection's `items` to a flat ordered `PostData[]`
- `getCollectionsForPost(postSlug)` — finds all collections containing a given post (runs at build time)

### URL helper (`urls.ts`)
- `getPostUrlInCollection(post, collectionSlug)` — appends `?collection=slug`

### Sidebar components (`PostSidebar`, `SeriesList`)
- Both read `?collection=` via `useSearchParams()` (client-side, no SSR conflict)
- If a matching collection is found in `collectionContexts`, the sidebar switches to show the collection's posts, label, and links — all preserving `?collection=` in navigation

### Post pages (`posts/[slug]`, `[slug]/[postSlug]`)
- Call `getCollectionsForPost` at build time and pass `collectionContexts` through to `PostLayout`
- `showSidebar` activates even for posts with no series if they appear in a collection

### Collection index page (`series/[slug]`)
- Detects `type: collection`, fetches via `getCollectionPosts`
- Shows "Collection" badge instead of "Series"
- "Start reading" link includes `?collection=slug`

### To create a collection, add `content/series/my-collection/index.mdx`:
```yaml
---
type: collection
title: "My Learning Path"
excerpt: "A curated reading path."
date: "2026-01-01"
items:
  - series: rust-basics
  - post: my-standalone-post
  - series: async-rust
    exclude:
      - async-intro
---
```

   218→function parseMarkdownFile(fullPath: string, slug: string, dateFromFileName?: string, seriesName?: string): PostData {
   219→  const fileContents = fs.readFileSync(fullPath, 'utf8');
   220→  const { data: rawData, content } = matter(fileContents);

   222→  const parsed = PostSchema.safeParse(rawData);
   223→  if (!parsed.success) {

   225→    throw new Error(`Invalid frontmatter in ${fullPath}`);

   228→
   229→  const contentWithoutH1 = content.replace(/^\s*#\s+[^\n]+/, '').trim();
   230→
   231→  const effectiveSeriesSlug = data.series || seriesName;
   232→  let authors: string[] = [];
   233→  if (data.authors && Array.isArray(data.authors)) {
   234→    authors = data.authors;
   235→  } else if (data.author) {
   236→    authors = [data.author];
   237→  } else {
   238→    // Inherit from series if this post belongs to one
   239→    if (effectiveSeriesSlug) {
   240→      const seriesAuthors = getSeriesAuthors(effectiveSeriesSlug);
   241→      if (seriesAuthors) {
   242→        authors = seriesAuthors;
   243→      }
   244→    }
   245→    if (authors.length === 0) {
   246→      const defaultAuthors = siteConfig.posts?.authors?.default;
   247→      if (defaultAuthors && defaultAuthors.length > 0) {
   248→        authors = defaultAuthors;
   249→      }
   250→    }
   251→  }
   252→
   253→  const excerpt = data.excerpt || generateExcerpt(contentWithoutH1);
   254→  const readingTime = calculateReadingTime(contentWithoutH1);
   255→  
   256→  let date = data.date;
   257→  if (!date && dateFromFileName) date = dateFromFileName;
   258→  if (!date) date = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]; // Fallback

   260→  const headings = getHeadings(content);

   265→    coverImage = `/posts/${slug}/${cleanPath}`;

When a collection contains a post, the sidebar and mobile navigation switch to collection-aware mode, showing the collection's posts in order and preserving navigation context via ?collection=slug.\n\nKey additions:\n- New `type: collection` and `items:` fields in series frontmatter\n- `getCollectionPosts()` resolves a collection's items to a flat ordered PostData[]\n- `getCollectionsForPost()` finds all collections containing a given post at build time\n- Client-side query param detection in PostSidebar and SeriesList for sidebar switching\n- Updated series index page to handle both regular series and collections\n- SeriesCatalog links respect collection context with ?collection= param\n\nIncludes sample \"Modern Web Development\" collection combining JS, React, and Next.js reading.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\"",

}

> AGENT

Two issues confirmed:

1. **`getAllSeries()` calls `getSeriesPosts()` for every slug** — collections return 0 since they use `items`, not `series` tagging on posts.
2. **`PostNavigation` (bottom prev/next cards)** — it's a server component receiving global adjacent posts from `getAdjacentPosts`. It has no knowledge of `?collection=`, so it shows the wrong neighbours when in collection context.

Let me fix both.

> TOOL

Fetch posts for each series, filtering out draft series in production\n  seriesSet.forEach(slug => {\n    const seriesData = getSeriesData(slug);\n    if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' && seriesData?.draft) {\n      return; // Skip draft series in production\n    }\n    series[slug] = getSeriesPosts(slug);\n  });",
  "new_string": "  // 3. Fetch posts for each series, filtering out draft series in production\n  seriesSet.forEach(slug => {\n    const seriesData = getSeriesData(slug);\n    if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' && seriesData?.draft) {\n      return; // Skip draft series in production\n    }\n    series[slug] = seriesData?.type === 'collection'\n      ? getCollectionPosts(slug)\n      : getSeriesPosts(slug);\n  });"
}

(collectionContexts ?? []).find(c => c.slug === collectionParam) ?? null\n    : null;\n\n  let effectivePrev = prev;\n  let effectiveNext = next;\n\n  if (activeCollection && currentSlug) {\n    const posts = activeCollection.posts;\n    const idx = posts.findIndex(p => p.slug === currentSlug);\n    effectivePrev = idx > 0 ? posts[idx - 1] : null;\n    effectiveNext = idx < posts.length - 1 ? posts[idx + 1] : null;\n  }\n\n  const postHref = (post: PostData) =>\n    activeCollection ? getPostUrlInCollection(post, activeCollection.slug) : getPostUrl(post);\n\n  if (!effectivePrev && !effectiveNext) return null;\n\n  return (\n    <nav\n      className=\"mt-12 pt-12 border-t border-muted/20 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3\"\n      aria-label={t('post_navigation')}\n    >\n      {effectivePrev && (\n        <Link\n          href={postHref(effectivePrev)}\n          className=\"group flex flex-col gap-1.5 p-4 rounded-xl border border-muted/15 hover:border-accent/30 hover:bg-accent/5 transition-all no-underline\"\n        >\n          <span className=\"text-[10px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-muted flex items-center gap-1.5\">\n            <svg className=\"w-3 h-3\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\" strokeWidth={2}>\n              <path strokeLinecap=\"round\" strokeLinejoin=\"round\" d=\"M15 19l-7-7 7-7\" />\n            </svg>\n            {t('prev')}\n          </span>\n          <span className=\"text-sm font-serif font-semibold text-heading group-hover:text-accent transition-colors line-clamp-2 leading-snug\">\n            {effectivePrev.title}\n          </span>\n          <span className=\"text-xs font-mono text-muted/60\">{effectivePrev.date}</span>\n        </Link>\n      )}\n\n      {effectiveNext && (\n        <Link\n          href={postHref(effectiveNext)}\n          className={`group flex flex-col gap-1.5 p-4 rounded-xl border border-muted/15 hover:border-accent/30 hover:bg-accent/5 transition-all no-underline sm:items-end sm:text-right${!effectivePrev ? ' sm:col-start-2' : ''}`}\n        >\n          <span className=\"text-[10px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-muted flex items-center gap-1.5\">\n            {t('next')}\n            <svg className=\"w-3 h-3\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\" strokeWidth={2}>\n              <path strokeLinecap=\"round\" strokeLinejoin=\"round\" d=\"M9 5l7 7-7 7\" />\n            </svg>\n          </span>\n          <span className=\"text-sm font-serif font-semibold text-heading group-hover:text-accent transition-colors line-clamp-2 leading-snug\">\n            {effectiveNext.title}\n          </span>\n          <span className=\"text-xs font-mono text-muted/60\">{effectiveNext.date}</span>\n        </Link>\n      )}\n    </nav>\n  );\n}"
}

> AGENT

Both fixes are in and tests pass. Summary:

**Fix 1 — Parts count zero on series list page:**  
`getAllSeries()` now calls `getCollectionPosts()` instead of `getSeriesPosts()` for collections, so the card correctly shows 4 parts.

**Fix 2 — Wrong prev/next cards:**  
`PostNavigation` is now a client component. It reads `?collection=` from `useSearchParams()` and, when in collection context, computes prev/next from the collection's post list instead of the global chronological order. Links also carry `?collection=` to maintain context.

    15→
    16→const POST_PAGE_SIZE = siteConfig.pagination.posts;
    17→const SERIES_PAGE_SIZE = siteConfig.pagination.series;
    18→
    19→/**
    20→ * Generates the static paths for all top-level pages at build time,
    21→ * plus any custom URL prefixes configured for posts or series.
    22→ */
    23→export async function generateStaticParams() {
    24→  const pages = getAllPages();
    25→  const params = pages.map((page) => ({ slug: page.slug }));
    26→
    27→  // Add custom posts basePath listing (e.g. /articles)
    28→  const basePath = getPostsBasePath();
    29→  if (basePath !== 'posts') {
    30→    params.push({ slug: basePath });
    31→  }
    32→
    33→  // Add series custom path listings (e.g. /weeklies)
    34→  for (const customPath of Object.values(getSeriesCustomPaths())) {
    35→    params.push({ slug: customPath });
    36→  }
    37→
    38→  return params;
    39→}
    40→
    41→export const dynamicParams = false;
    42→
    43→export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }): Promise<Metadata> {
    44→  const { slug: rawSlug } = await params;
    45→  const slug = decodeURIComponent(rawSlug);
    46→
    47→  // Custom posts basePath
    48→  const basePath = getPostsBasePath();
    49→  if (slug === basePath && basePath !== 'posts') {
    50→    return {
    51→      title: `${t('posts')} | ${resolveLocale(siteConfig.title)}`,
    52→      description: t('posts_description'),
    53→    };
    54→  }
    55→
    56→  // Series custom paths
    57→  const customPaths = getSeriesCustomPaths();
    58→  const matchedSeriesSlug = Object.entries(customPaths).find(([, path]) => path === slug)?.[0];
    59→  if (matchedSeriesSlug) {
    60→    const seriesData = getSeriesData(matchedSeriesSlug);
    61→    if (seriesData) {
    62→      return {
    63→        title: `${seriesData.title} - ${t('series')} | ${resolveLocale(siteConfig.title)}`,
    64→        description: seriesData.excerpt,
    65→      };
    66→    }
    67→  }
    68→
    69→  const page = getPageBySlug(slug);
    70→  if (!page) {
    71→    return { title: 'Page Not Found' };
    72→  }
    73→
    74→  return {
    75→    title: `${page.title} | ${resolveLocale(siteConfig.title)}`,
    76→    description: page.excerpt,
    77→  };
    78→}
    79→
    80→export default async function Page({
    81→  params,
    82→}: {
    83→  params: Promise<{ slug: string }>;
    84→}) {
    85→  const { slug: rawSlug } = await params;
    86→  const slug = decodeURIComponent(rawSlug);
    87→
    88→  // Check if slug matches custom posts basePath
    89→  const basePath = getPostsBasePath();
    90→  if (slug === basePath && basePath !== 'posts') {
    91→    const allPosts = getListingPosts();
    92→    const totalPages = Math.ceil(allPosts.length / POST_PAGE_SIZE);
    93→    const posts = allPosts.slice(0, POST_PAGE_SIZE);
    94→
    95→    return (
    96→      <div className="layout-main">
    97→        <PageHeader
    98→          titleKey="posts"
    99→          subtitleKey="posts_subtitle"
   100→          subtitleParams={{ count: allPosts.length }}
   101→          className="mb-12"
   102→        />
   103→        <PostList posts={posts} />
   104→        {totalPages > 1 && (
   105→          <div className="mt-12">
   106→            <Pagination currentPage={1} totalPages={totalPages} basePath={`/${basePath}`} />
   107→          </div>
   108→        )}
   109→      </div>
   110→    );
   111→  }
   112→
   113→  // Check if slug matches a series custom path
   114→  const customPaths = getSeriesCustomPaths();
   115→  const matchedSeriesSlug = Object.entries(customPaths).find(([, path]) => path === slug)?.[0];
   116→  if (matchedSeriesSlug) {
   117→    const seriesData = getSeriesData(matchedSeriesSlug);
   118→    const allPosts = getSeriesPosts(matchedSeriesSlug);
   119→
   120→    if ((!seriesData && allPosts.length === 0) || (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' && seriesData?.draft)) {
   121→      notFound();
   122→    }
   123→
   124→    const totalPages = Math.ceil(allPosts.length / SERIES_PAGE_SIZE);
   125→    const posts = allPosts.slice(0, SERIES_PAGE_SIZE);
   126→
   127→    const title = seriesData?.title || matchedSeriesSlug.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + matchedSeriesSlug.slice(1);
   128→    const description = seriesData?.excerpt;
   129→    const coverImage = seriesData?.coverImage;
   130→
   131→    const explicitAuthors = getSeriesAuthors(matchedSeriesSlug);
   132→    let authors: string[];
   133→    if (explicitAuthors) {
   134→      authors = explicitAuthors;
   135→    } else if (allPosts.length > 0) {
   136→      const counts = new Map<string, number>();
   137→      for (const post of allPosts) {
   138→        for (const author of post.authors) {
   139→          counts.set(author, (counts.get(author) || 0) + 1);
   140→        }
   141→      }
   142→      authors = [...counts.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).map(([name]) => name);
   143→    } else {
   144→      authors = [];
   145→    }
   146→
   147→    return (
   148→      <div className="layout-main">
   149→        <header className="mb-16">
   150→          {coverImage && (
   151→            <div className="relative w-full h-56 md:h-72 mb-10 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-xl shadow-accent/5">
   152→              <CoverImage
   153→                src={coverImage}
   154→                title={title}
   155→                slug={matchedSeriesSlug}
   156→                className="w-full h-full object-cover"
   157→              />
   158→              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-background/60 to-transparent" />
   159→            </div>
   160→          )}
   161→          <div className="text-center max-w-2xl mx-auto">
   162→            <span className="badge-accent mb-4">
   163→              {t('series')} • {allPosts.length} {t('parts')}
   164→            </span>
   165→            <h1 className="page-title mb-4">{title}</h1>
   166→            {description && (
   167→              <p className="text-lg text-muted font-serif italic leading-relaxed">{description}</p>
   168→            )}
   169→            {authors.length > 0 && (
   170→              <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted">
   171→                <span className="mr-1">{t('written_by')}</span>
   172→                {authors.map((author, index) => (
   173→                  <span key={author}>
   174→                    <Link
   175→                      href={`/authors/${getAuthorSlug(author)}`}
   176→                      className="text-foreground hover:text-accent no-underline transition-colors duration-200"
   177→                    >
   178→                      {author}
   179→                    </Link>
   180→                    {index < authors.length - 1 && <span className="mr-1">,</span>}
   181→                  </span>
   182→                ))}
   183→              </p>
   184→            )}
   185→          </div>
   186→        </header>
   187→        <SeriesCatalog posts={posts} totalPosts={allPosts.length} />
   188→        {totalPages > 1 && (
   189→          <div className="mt-12">
   190→            <Pagination currentPage={1} totalPages={totalPages} basePath={`/${slug}`} />
   191→          </div>
   192→        )}
   193→      </div>
   194→    );
   195→  }
   196→
   197→  // Default: static page
   198→  const page = getPageBySlug(slug);
   199→
   200→  if (!page) {
   201→    notFound();
   202→  }
   203→
   204→  // Determine layout based on frontmatter, defaulting to 'simple' for pages
   205→  const layout = page.layout || 'simple';
   206→
   207→  if (layout === 'post') {
   208→    return <PostLayout post={page} commentCategory="staticPages" />;
   209→  }
   210→
   211→  return <SimpleLayout post={page} />;
   212→}
